## 2. Prepared tree on sp8, as bin/spark build prepare does (shim/prepare.sh): git fetch --filter=blob:none

## 3. Arms (git worktrees of $B/vllm-c50)
# no1009: c50-0777 + git am 0779, 0780.  Its four non-test files equal candidate-50/no1009/overlay byte for byte.
git worktree add --detach $B/vllm-no1009 c50-0777; git am 0779-*.patch 0780-*.patch
# fix: c50-0777 + git am candidate-50's 0778, then shim/apply_fix.py (attention.py) and shim/apply_test.py
# (tests/models/test_deepseek_v4_1_attention.py), amended into 0778's commit (author and date kept,
# committer date = author date, message shim/commit-msg.txt), then git am 0779, 0780.
git worktree add -b rework-1009-r1 $B/vllm-fix c50-0777
git am 0778-*.patch; python3 ../shim/apply_fix.py; python3 ../shim/apply_test.py
git add -A; GIT_COMMITTER_DATE="$(git log -1 --format=%ad)" git commit --amend -F ../shim/commit-msg.txt; git tag fix-0778
git am 0779-*.patch 0780-*.patch
git format-patch -1 fix-0778 --stdout > 0778-perf-dsv41-sp-prefill-local-attention-front.patch
git diff c50 HEAD > 0778-r1-vs-candidate-50.diff
# Fresh-tree check of the delivered patch (raw/verify-stack.txt): c50-0777 + git am the delivered 0778,
# 0779, 0780: all apply; git diff --check 19f2c20ed4d6..HEAD clean; tree 638711070bbe = vllm-fix's tree.
